22/01/26, 11:40 pm - Rahul: Here’s a **concrete, concise comparison** of **Plivo vs SignalWire** for your call-reminder app, including **voice call rates, phone number costs, and rotation planning**.

---

## 📞 Provider Pricing Summary

### **1) Plivo (Good for India & quick setup)**

**Voice call rate (India outbound):**

* ~₹0.40 per minute for outbound calls. Billing is per 60-second interval, so a 20-second call still bills as 1 minute. ([plivo.com][1])

**Phone number (DID) cost:**

* Local Indian number: around **₹250/month** (approx.) for each number. ([plivo.com][1])

**Pros:**

* Easy India number provisioning
* Stable for transactional reminders
* Lower blocking risk when behaviour controls applied
* Simple pay-as-you-go

**Cons:**

* Higher per-minute compared to some global providers
* Billing per full minute (20 sec still counts as 60 sec) ([support.plivo.com][2])

---

### **2) SignalWire (Cheapest voice, best at scale)**

**Voice call rate (outbound):**

* Local outbound: ~$0.008/min (~₹0.65/min approx at ₹80/$). ([signalwire.com][3])

**Phone number rental cost:**

* Local DID: **$0.50/month (~₹40–45)**
* Toll-Free DID: **$0.80/month (~₹65–70)** ([signalwire.com][3])

**Pros:**

* Very low call cost
* Lower number cost
* Good for high volume

**Cons:**

* India local DID support may be limited or require workarounds (provider focuses on U.S./Canada numbers)
* Setup & compliance for India may need extra steps

---

## 📊 Quick Cost Comparison (Voice + DID)

| Provider       |   Voice Cost / min | DID Cost / number / month | Best Fit                        |
| -------------- | -----------------: | ------------------------: | ------------------------------- |
| **Plivo**      |             ~₹0.40 |                     ~₹250 | India-centric reminders         |
| **SignalWire** | ~₹0.65 (converted) |                   ~₹40–70 | High volume English/U.S. region |

> Note: SignalWire voice rates vary by route type (e.g., SIP ~$0.003/min too). ([signalwire.com][3])

---

## 📈 How Many Numbers You Need (Rotation)

You **do not** need to rotate numbers every month. Instead use **small stable pool** with **health-based rotation**:

### Basic Rotation Rule

* **Start with 2–3 active numbers**
* **1 standby number** (idle, clean)
* Monitor number health:

  * Rotate only if block rate rises
  * Do not replace every month automatically

### Approx Plan Based on Users

| Daily Call Load        | Estimated Numbers |
| ---------------------- | ----------------: |
| Up to ~1,500 calls/day |                 1 |
| ~3,000–5,000 calls/day |               1–2 |
| ~10,000 calls/day      |               2–3 |

**Rotation Guidance:**

* Rotate number only when necessary based on block/health signals
* Keep numbers alive longer by controlling call frequency and patterns

---

## 🧠 Which One to Choose

### When to pick **Plivo**

* Your app is India-focused
* You want easy +91 number buying
* You want minimal compliance headaches
* Reminder calls are short transactional calls

### When to use **SignalWire**

* You are targeting **US/International numbers**
* You need **lowest voice cost at scale**
* You are prepared to handle compliance yourself
* You plan to mix AI agents or SIP calls

---

## 🪙 Practical Per-User Cost Example (India focus)

For a reminder system billing calls:

* **20 sec call ≈ 60 sec bill**
* **Plivo ~₹0.40 / call**

If user gets ~2 calls/week:

```
2 calls × 4 weeks × ₹0.40 ≈ ₹3.20/user/month
```

Phone numbers:

* 2 numbers × ₹250 = ₹500/month
* Spread over many users, this becomes negligible per user.

---

## 📌 Summary

**Best choice for your call reminder app:**

* **Plivo** for India (easy numbers, stable compliance)
* **SignalWire** if you expand beyond India or want lowest voice cost at scale

**Phone numbers:**

* Pay monthly rental (~₹250 with Plivo, ~$0.50 with SignalWire)
* Use a small pool (2–3) with monitoring
* Rotate only on health signals

---

If you want, I can give you a **sample monthly cost calculator template** (Excel/Google Sheet model) for different user volumes.

Phone number (India DID):
~₹75–120 per number per month
Rotation (aapke use case ke liye):
1k–5k users → 1–2 numbers
10k users → 3–5 numbers
Rule:
Monthly rotate mat karo
Sirf tab rotate karo jab number ki health ya block rate badhne lage
Kyun affordable hai:
Low per-user daily cost
Cheap numbers
India-friendly compliance
Account suspend hone ka risk kam
2️⃣ SignalWire – Cheap hai, lekin India-first ke liye ideal nahi
Voice rate:
~₹0.08–0.25 per minute (dikhne mein sasta lagta hai)
Phone number:
~₹40 per month
Problem:
India local numbers limited hain
Routing aur compliance ka headache
Ye tab sahi hai jab aap zyada calls US ya international mein kar rahe ho
Conclusion:
India main market hai toh risk hai
3️⃣ Exotel – Reminder app ke liye affordable nahi
Voice rate (outgoing):
₹1.00 per minute
(2-leg billing lagta hai, matlab aur mehenga padta hai)
Phone numbers:
10 free numbers milte hain
Extra number → ₹499 per month
Fixed cost:
~₹58,400 per year upfront
Kyun mehenga hai:
Plivo se 2–3 times zyada cost
Call center type business ke liye bana hai
Daily reminder apps ke liye overkill hai
Per User Per Day Cost (Daily Call Scenario)
Plivo: ~₹0.40 → Best
SignalWire: ~₹0.25–0.65 → India risk
Exotel: ₹1.00+ → Too expensive
Final Recommendation
Start aur scale ke liye: Plivo use karo
Numbers plan:
Start with 2 numbers
Har ~5,000 daily calls pe 1 number add karo
Monthly rotation mat karo
Sirf tab rotate karo jab block rate badhne lage
One-line Decision
Plivo sabse affordable aur sustainable choice hai aapke reminder app ke liye.
SignalWire future mein international traffic ke liye theek hai.
Exotel daily/weekly reminder calls ke liye cost-effective nahi hai.
Agar aap chaho, next main:
1k / 5k / 10k users ka exact monthly cost calculate kar sakta hoon
Plivo-only architecture design kar sakta hoon
Number long-term kaise safe rakhein, wo strategy dikha sakta hoon
